Workflow lesson, 2026-07-02: Weber's completed 20-page sample-cert shows that page-unit sampling is necessary even after compile-clean and map-phase/agent labels. Equations can look acceptable while connective prose, theorem structure, summation conventions, and source notation have been compressed or modernized. For public records, labels such as `verified faithful`, `source-checked`, or `complete` must be stress-tested by adversarial sample pages before being presented as range-level claims.

Published 2026-06-24: `GibbsV1_P3_p125_134.zip` extends Paper 3 to printed pp.125-134 and cumulative pp.055-134 with TeX/PDF/source scans, formula/table/scan-map ledgers, clean compile QC, and repair log. A separate local source-quality refresh downloaded IA raw JP2/scandata for the 1906 Scientific Papers Vols. I-II and a separate processed-JP2/PDF witness for `On the Equilibrium of Heterogeneous Substances`; future continuation should use those image-level witnesses rather than relying only on derivative PDFs. Top-level PDFs/TeX are reader-facing working drafts; source-scan slices and method/audit files are preserved in compact ZIP packets. This is a source-scan-backed working tranche, not a complete Gibbs corpus or final critical edition. Continue after p.134. |
| Bianchi | Current dedicated Bianchi record published as <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20615814>. `Lezioni di geometria differenziale` Vol. I has top-level Italian source transcription, corrected English translation working draft, source-scan witness, and TeX/auditfix package through source pdfpages 001-543. The A2 branch now has compact/core Italian-English working coverage through source p0135 via `Bianchi_A2_core_p0001_0135_IT_EN_20260613.zip`; earlier large scan-heavy p0105/repair packages remain provenance/backstop layers. Local pending note 2026-06-13: a smaller same-name Edge re-export of the p0135 core package was routed as `Bianchi_A2_core_p0001_0135_IT_EN_20260613_EDGE_5MB_NAME_COLLISION.zip`; it is pending review/upload and is not a public catalog replacement until a new Zenodo version is published. A local Bianchi continuation handoff adds useful A1/A2 source-control and upload-order notes, but its "A2 not started" statement is stale relative to the staged p0135 A2 working packages. A2 is not complete; TeX build/render success is not glyph-level certification, and matrix dots, prime marks, summation superscripts, and handwritten-symbol details still need continuing source audit. OCR/image-analysis outputs remain witness/locator layers only. |
| Gordan / Clebsch-Gordan | Current dedicated Gordan/Clebsch-Gordan record published as <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20822196> under concept DOI <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20616260>. The 2026-06-24 version adds `Gordan_Theta_1863_FullAudit_fix06_20260623.zip`, `Gordan_Abelsche_FinalAuditFix02_DE_EN_20260613.zip`, `Gordan_de_linea_p025_047_final_LA_EN_scans_20260613.zip`, `Gordan_VB1_01_p001_009_DE_EN_20260613.zip`, and `Gordan_VB1_02_p010_028_DE_EN_20260613.zip`. This makes the FinalAuditFix02, De linea p001-p047, theta fix06, and Vorlesungen Bd.1 p001-p028 packets public. Earlier Abel tranches and AllPrior/AuditFix packets remain correction/provenance layers. These are source-witnessed working drafts and source-audit packets, not certified critical editions; OCR remains a locator/check layer and important formulas, diagrams, notation, and theorem statements must be checked against included source witnesses. The full Gordan article corpus register remains open, and Vorlesungen Bd.1 is only begun through source p28. |
| Frobenius | Current dedicated record published as <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20673444> (latest version <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20821858>). The public surface includes `Frobenius_all_GE_EN_cum_scans_QA03_20260611.zip`, `Frobenius_all_GE_EN_cum_scans_RA05_20260613.zip`, and top-level German/English cumulative PDF/TeX readers. The selected group-character sequence covers items 053, 054, 056, 057, 058, 059, 060, 061, 070, and 071, with 10/10 selected items, 221/221 tracked source-intake pages, 241 cumulative source-scan pages, German cumulative reader of 182 pages, and English cumulative reader of 176 pages. RA05 fixes English item 070 formula punctuation, replaces non-compilable cumulative source-archive TeX with directly compilable cumulative TeX/PDF, passes ZIP extraction verification, and reports zero German/English structural/formula/build flags. Working/source-witnessed draft only; not a certified critical edition. |
| Poincare | Current dedicated record published as <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20673461>. Initial dedicated split publishes `poincare_v1_*` working packages through public `poincare_v1_26.zip`. This is explicitly non-continuous: current public files include v1_01, v1_02, v1_08-v1_21, and v1_24-v1_26. A 2026-06-25 Edge-download sweep recovered local but not-yet-public v1_03, v1_04, v1_05, and v1_07 packages; v1_06 and v1_22-v1_23 remain absent by this sweep. Use package-by-package audit notes rather than treating it as a seamless Tome I edition. |
| Steinitz | Current dedicated Steinitz record published as <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20616988> under concept DOI <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20616988>. The standalone split includes `Steinitz_8_1910_fields_1-24_complete_shortpaths.zip`, `Steinitz_34_core_shortpaths.zip`, `Steinitz_41_strict_1894_1897_1906_shortpaths.zip`, `Steinitz_43_bedingtII_complete_shortpaths.zip`, and `Steinitz_44_bedingtIII_p001-013_shortpaths.zip`. Public scope: 1910 fields sections 1-24 represented as German/English working TeX/PDF; 1913 `Bedingt konvergente Reihen und konvexe Systeme` I represented; strict/manual 1894 dissertation, 1897 configuration paper, and 1906 Euler polyhedron-relations paper represented; 1914 `Bedingt konvergente Reihen und konvexe Systeme` II represented through pp.1-66; and 1916 `Bedingt konvergente Reihen und konvexe Systeme` III started through pp.1-13. Known gaps remain explicit: 1908 Analysis Situs now has a locally staged 22-page Ranicki offprint witness, but only as provisional 300-ppi source-intake rather than strict 650+ certification; the remainder of 1916 Bedingt III, 1916/1922 polyhedron-space-division work, and 1927/1928 isoperimetric papers are not completed. Treat as package-audited working/source-witnessed drafts, not final critical editions. Local project-upload sweep 2026-06-24 added a separate Steinitz ChatGPT Project source/control set, not a promoted Zenodo reader update: 23 files, about 3.09 GB, with split 600dpi high-resolution page-image witnesses for most staged remaining work. It restates the live continuation point as 1916 Bedingt III source p.14, estimates about 478/713 handoff-produced pages (~67%), and warns that this is only about 60-65% canonical confidence until older accepted batches receive fresh source audits. The 1908 Analysis Situs witness remains a provisional ~300ppi Ranicki offprint. |
| Kneser | Current dedicated Kneser record published as <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20836971> (latest version <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20836972>). The public surface fronts German-source and English working-translation PDF/TeX readers through p0011-p0248, a high-quality source witness through p0001-p0248, and the p0234 lower-p0248 slice/audit package. Worklist reports 248/336 source pages done (73.8%), latest slice sections 53-55 completing the Sixth Section, and next continuation at p0249 / Seventh Section / section 56. Source-witnessed working draft and audit/progress record only; not a certified critical edition. |
| Additional author cluster | Current clean author-cluster record published as <https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20411006>. This is now a mixed backstop/provenance shelf for routed authors not yet split into standalone records and older copies. Kneser, Poincare, and Frobenius now have preferred standalone records above; their older copies in mixed shelves remain as backstop/provenance where present. Mikami, Kronecker/Kron, Picard, Klein-Fricke, and related tranches still need author-level triage or cleaner dedicated rollups. Seki is tracked primarily under the non-European/Japanese lane until a compact reader-facing rollup exists. See [author page candidates](../docs/author-page-candidates.md) for the human-readability split queue. |
| Secondary routed lanes | The Edge/local sweep also routed and extracted useful but not-yet-author-page-ready packets for Mikami, Picard, Kronecker/Kron, and Klein-Fricke. Mikami 1913 now has an EN/JA p001-p059 corrective diagram package with p0017, p0019, and p0050 diagrams rebuilt as explicit TikZ and all pages contact-sheet checked. Picard Tome I has a figure-refined FR/EN package through source leaf 107, including Fig. 1--7 TikZ correction and new leaves 101--107. Kronecker has a first DE/EN production slice for `GrundzÃ¼ge einer arithmetischen Theorie der algebraischen GrÃ¶ssen` covering half-title, dedication, contents, prefatory opening, and Part I Â§Â§1--3. Klein-Fricke Volume I has an authorship-clean correction through PDF pages 1--42: visible provenance labels are removed from reader PDFs and moved to ledgers, while source-page boundary commands remain in TeX. |
| GitHub push | Local mirror is updated and SSH authentication works for the configured key; public status/caveat updates are being pushed to <https://github.com/KokunoYumeto/modern-latex-manuscripts>. Corrections and proposed improvements can be suggested through GitHub issues or pull requests. |
